Measured live at 1440×900: six windows open, none auto-closing, each **898px = 62% of viewport**, while the widest table needs **3333px** — the operator saw roughly **27% of the table** while **38% of the screen sat empty** as backdrop behind a floating window, in an app whose main content is a 15-column, 9,096-row grid. No new concept was needed (`MAXIMIZE`, `state: 'max'` and the tab-id `Set` idiom all existed); only the **initial state** of some windows changed, keeping `prevRect` so Restore has somewhere to go. Reading the code turned up a separate bug: the singleton branch of `OPEN` forced `state: 'normal'`, so **re-clicking a sidebar item shrank an already-maximized window**. #289 then fixed the _shape_ of the first fix — #286 used an **allowlist** covering 9 of 28 tabs and the operator immediately reported 13 screens still floating, so the rule was **inverted**: `FLOATING_BY_DEFAULT` names the exceptions and everything else maximizes, with a test pinning that a hypothetical **future** tab id maximizes. That inversion matters more than the patch: an allowlist would have silently handed the same gap to whoever adds the next data screen. #290 removed the last two — Pricing (Std) and (Cpx) had been left floating on my own reasoning that "comparing Standard beside Complex is why MDI exists", which was **speculation, not a requirement**; the operator said the opposite (each is a dense multi-tab form whose right edge a 900px window cuts off, and in practice people work one quote at a time). The 4 surviving exceptions (`ink-calc`, `print-area`, `design-tools`, `messages`) share a reason the two pricing screens never had: they are opened **beside** a quote to feed a number **back into** it, so covering the worksheet defeats the purpose. Final split **24 / 28** maximized. Both tests that used `'standard'` as the example of "an ordinary floating window" were repointed at `ink-calc` **with the reason written down** — not loosened to suit the new behaviour; what they assert (a floating window has `state: 'normal'` and `prevRect: null`) is unchanged. **Plan 3 — Carbon convergence** (#287 `73e4a1f`, docs-only, not one line of JSX restyled). Three design languages coexisted: `DESIGN.md` described an **Apple** system (`#000`/`#f5f5f7`, Apple Blue `#0071e3`, SF Pro, 980px pill buttons), the shipped app is **IBM Carbon** (navy, square corners, IBM Plex Mono, `#0f62fe`), and the token file is a **Tailwind** slate/gray scale — with the top hardcoded hex values mixing both palettes. Documentation that does not match the app is more dangerous than none: a newcomer builds faithfully to the Apple spec and creates a **fourth** visual language. So this wave removed only the sources of drift — the Apple spec was **archived, not deleted** (`docs/design/ARCHIVED-2026-09-10-apple-design-system.md`, with a banner saying it does not describe the running app; it records a real decision that was weighed and set aside), `DESIGN.md` was rewritten short and Carbon pointing at the three real token files, and exactly **one** token was renamed: `--color-slate-900` held `#0f2341` while real Tailwind slate-900 is `#0f172a`. The **value is correct and deliberate** — it unified `#0e2550`, `#162c52`, `#243352`, `#1a3a5c`, as the file itself records — and only the **name** lied; one wrong entry discredits the whole ramp, because a reasonable reader assumes Tailwind values and picks the wrong colour. Renamed `--color-brand-navy`. **Plan 4 — nav + i18n cleanup** (#288 `4a2ac76`, #291 `d508dfe`; its third part became wave 1 of the translation project — see Sprint S-I18N-WAVES-1-10 above). The sidebar showed **"RFQ Tracking"** under _Quoting & Pricing_ and **"RFQ Tracker"** under _Tracking_, with Home's Quick Action saying "RFQ Tracker" — nothing told an English reader which screen would open. **Vietnamese was already correct** (`Danh sách RFQ` / `Theo dõi RFQ`), so English followed Vietnamese instead of inventing new words: **RFQ List** and **RFQ Progress**, applied to all seven surfaces carrying the old labels (`nav.tab.*`, the `TopBar` breadcrumb, the `CostModule` MDI window title + taskbar, `rfq_tracking.title`, `home.qa.rfq`, `RfqTracking.jsx` `datasetLabel`, `help/content.js`). **Tab ids left alone** — they are serialized into saved window layouts, so renaming an id orphans a user's layout. The PR states plainly that its accompanying test **passes both before and after**: two labels are _confusable_, not _identical_, and no honest automated check defines "confusable" without an arbitrary threshold — the rename is a judgment about wording, not test-driven, written up as such rather than manufacturing a fake red test. #291 then fixed what only looking at the screen could catch: the new login language toggle rendered at the **top-right of the page**, not inside the login card, while every test passed, the build was clean, and #288 had already been declared done. The CSS had been written for `.login-card` — **dead CSS, zero uses in any JSX** — while the real card is `form.cb-card` with `position: static`, so the absolutely-positioned toggle escaped up to the nearest positioned ancestor, `.cb-shell` (`position: fixed`), i.e. the whole page. The plan **did** say to make the card a positioned ancestor if it was not one; it was checked — **on the wrong element**, and the check passed for a class that is never rendered. Fixed with `position: relative` on `.cb-card` plus a comment naming what depends on it, and verified against the real DOM (`cardPosition: relative`, `insideCard: true`, `toggleInsideCardBounds: true`). **Pattern across this sprint worth carrying**: #289, #290 and #291 — and later #309 and #314 in the i18n sprint — were each found by operating the running or installed app, never by the suite. That is exactly what Lesson 37's verify-loop exists for.
>
> **Sprint S-FIRSTRUN-IPC — the CLIENT first-run dialog could never reach the main process, fixed 2026-09-10 (SHA: `d5ed84a` via PR #279).** Henry's CLIENT build answered `✗ Không kết nối được: probe timeout` for every URL, including a SERVER that Safari on the same Mac reached fine. **Root cause**: the dialog is rendered from a `data:text/html` URL, which has an **opaque origin**. Its renderer called `fetch('/__probe__?url=…')` expecting `session.webRequest.onBeforeRequest` to intercept the sentinel — but a **root-relative URL cannot be resolved against a non-hierarchical base**, so WHATWG URL parsing fails, the request is never issued, `onBeforeRequest` never fires and `probeServer()` is never called. The timeout message was the dialog's own 4 s fallback, not a network result. All three buttons (Test / Save / Skip) were dead the same way — S-WIZARD-CORS below had fixed the CORS symptom with a mechanism that cannot work from an opaque origin at all. **Fix**: extract the dialog to `desktop/clientFirstRun.js` and talk over `ipcRenderer.invoke` against three `ipcMain.handle` channels (`ops:firstrun.testServer` / `.save` / `.skip`) — exactly what `setupWizard.js` was already doing correctly. Handlers are removed on `win.on('closed')` so reopening the dialog cannot double-register. **This removes the last `__probe__` / `__firstrun__` sentinel: `webRequest.onBeforeRequest` interception is gone from the codebase.** **Tests**: `desktop/clientFirstRun.test.js` + `desktop/setupWizard.test.js` drive a real `BrowserWindow` in Electron (`test:firstrun` / `test:wizard`) — the first test surface this wrapper has ever had, which is why the dead sentinel survived three months. Also added `markIncomplete()` to `setupWizard.js`, and `clientFirstRun.js` to `desktop/package.json` `build.files` (else it is absent from the packaged app).
>

Necessary coupling handled: dropped the `OPS_KIOSK_KEY` requirement from `server/index.js` boot + `scripts/preflight-env.js` (else a fresh install fails to boot). **Shared edits** (kept the file, removed refs): App/main/Sidebar/sidebarSections/sectionDefs/strings/api/CostModule/tabPreload/AppConfig (client); `index.js` (mounts + `/api/runtime-config features` now just `alt_materials` + boot check) + `sidebarVisibility.js` server mirror (drift test stays green: client==server ids); package.json (kiosk scripts, `apps/kiosk` in build/test, jest ignores, dead `seed:mes`/`migrate`), `ci.yml` (kiosk step), `desktop/main.js` (`OPS_KIOSK_KEY` gen), `desktop/package.json` (`../domains` extraResources), `eslint.config.js` (kiosk blocks). **Orphans the deletion created** (caught by `deadCode.lint.test.js`): removed `ReasonCodeFormModal`/`ReasonCodeDisableModal` + planning-only `utils/fieldMap.js` + `utils/routingHours.js`(+test); fixed stale `decimalInputBudget` + rbac + http.integration test entries. **Left intentionally** (harmless, no deleted imports): `scripts/import-fallback-xlsx.js` WO-mode (standalone dev script, now permanently gated-off; its test verifies the graceful skip). **Verify**: client 1528/1528, server 1026/1026 (+1 chronic skip), ESLint 0 errors, Vite tree-shook clean (no unresolved), perf-budget green — bundle **shrank ~98 kB (2377→2279) / 12 fewer chunks** (cross-link MES-3-FIX-62). One PR.

> **Sprint S-2FA-RESET — SYS-only per-user 2FA reset + Modal focus fix + desktop "Server connecting" screen shipped 2026-07-01/02.** Operators who lose their phone needed a 2FA reset without the all-or-nothing `npm run reset-totp` (nukes everyone). New `POST /api/auth/users/:id/reset-2fa` — `isSys` gate (STRICTER than the legacy admin+ DELETE), step-up password (`checkPassword` on the CALLER, not target), no self-reset, race-safe `updateTotpSecrets` (never raw file ops), revoke target sessions, `TOTP_RESET_BY_SYS` audit; the legacy `DELETE /totp/secret/:username` gained its missing audit row. Client: shield "Reset 2FA" button (`!isMe && isSys`) → confirm + step-up modal → `resetUser2fa`. **Shipped in TWO PRs** because the client UI landed first while the server was still uncommitted in the working tree (main was briefly inconsistent — button 404'd + raw i18n keys): client UI + Modal fix + desktop screen via **PR #228 (`aa8cbba`)**; server route + api wrapper + `settings.reset2fa.*` i18n (EN+VI) + `.acct-reset2fa*` CSS + `reset2fa.integration.test.js` (7 cases) via **PR #229 (`a6dd5eb`)**. Passed `/security-review` (no HIGH/MEDIUM). **Two fixes bundled in #228**: (1) shared `<Modal>` autofocus effect no longer depends on the inline `onClose` prop — it re-ran on every keystroke and stole focus from body inputs ("gõ 1 ký tự bị bật"); fixed via `onCloseRef` + deps `[open, dismissable]` (**Lesson 38**), plus `draggable` on the Reset 2FA modal (Lesson 36). (2) desktop `createMainWindow` now shows a "Đang kết nối máy chủ…" data-URL spinner + polls `/health` via `probeServer` (backoff to 60s) before the ERR_CONNECTION_REFUSED recovery dialog, extracted to `showConnectFailedDialog` (**MES-3-FIX-60**; incident root cause was a double-launch `launchctl bootstrap` + `open` racing port 3100 — verify-loop install uses bootstrap ONLY). Verify: client 1468/1468 + server 2FA 7/7 (isolated DATA_DIR per Lesson 33) + hardware-installed SERVER DMG (`ea4950ac…`, health 200, clean boot). **Follow-up MES-3-FIX-61**: the legacy self-reset-without-code hole on `DELETE /totp/secret/:username` (only the audit row was added; the self path still needs a current-TOTP / step-up gate). **CLOSED 2026-07-22 (`03945d8` via PR #242)** — read this sentence as the state at ship time.

> **Sprint B3e — requireTabAccess defense-in-depth on mt-import + print-area/upload shipped 2026-06-19 (SHA: `4710c4d` via PR #174).** Phase B group B3e of the pre-go-live audit (A4-02 finding). Two `multer`-fronted upload routes in `server/routes/shared.js` were missing the symmetric `requireTabAccess(...)` middleware that the rfq-tracker + sample-tracking attachment routes already use (lines 925, 995, 1094, 1912, 1976, 2069 — verified via Lesson 7 grep). Defense-in-depth gap, NOT auth hole: both handlers already invoke `requireWriter()` + `isAdminPlus()` inline so unauthorized callers were rejected before this change. The new middleware tightens the rejection point to BEFORE multer parses the multipart body — no file is processed for unauthorized requests (fail-fast + tmpdir cleanup unaffected since handlers still run the inline best-effort `fs.unlinkSync` on `req.file` as before). **Tab id Lesson 7 save**: my first instinct for `/machine-technical/:kind/import` was `requireTabAccess('machine-technical')` — but `grep -nE "id: 'lib-" client/src/components/Layout/Sidebar.jsx` showed the canonical sidebar id is `'lib-machine-tech'` (NOT `'machine-technical'`). If I had trusted the route URL, every operator's request would have hit the default-`'edit'` fallback in `resolveTabAccess` so the change would have been a no-op cosmetic; correct tab id ties the middleware to the actual permission-group surface. Print-area tab id `'print-area'` confirmed via Sidebar.jsx:24. **Permission-group default**: `resolveTabAccess` returns `'edit'` for unrecognized tab ids, so users without a `lib-machine-tech` / `print-area` entry in their `tab_permissions` map keep their existing access. Operators with explicit `'read'` or `'hidden'` on these tabs WILL be tightened — intended (bulk-import should require library edit access, not just generic writer role). **Verify**: full server suite 859/860 pass + 1 skip (MES-3-FIX-52 chronic, pre-existing). node :3100 boot + curl unauth — POST /api/shared/machine-technical/printing/import → 401, POST /api/shared/print-area/upload → 401, GET /health → 200. **Spec escalation gate did NOT trigger**: if curl had revealed a previously-unguarded route (200 unauth), I was instructed to STOP and escalate; both routes returned 401, confirming pre-existing auth via inline `requireWriter`. **Phase B closed end-to-end** with this PR — all 5 follow-up groups (B3a/b/c/d/e + B1/B2 doc refresh from 2026-06-18) shipped across PRs #166/167/169/171/172/173/174 + sprint history entries via #168/170 + this one. Remaining deferred items (P3 from Phase A): A1-14 formula-count nit, A2-20 Cpx exhaustive help re-audit, A4-09 DateCell aria-label. Post-go-live tickets: MES-3-FIX-56 (Cpx dedup, P2 D+14 sprint), MES-3-FIX-57 (seed-admin dev-init, P3), MES-3-FIX-59 (multer bump, P2 after 2026-08-29 freeze-end).

No code change. Phase A audit verified ZERO of the 3 npm-audit findings (multer high × 2 + uuid moderate cascading to exceljs) are practically exploitable in our deployed topology: all 8 multer instances behind `requireRole(4)` / `requireTabAccess(...)` / `isSys(u)` gates with 10–200 MB size caps; uuid's missing-bounds-check exploit path is unreachable because our code never invokes `uuid` with a pre-supplied `buf`. Bumping `multer@<2.2.0 → ≥2.2.0` is a minor bump but spans 8 operator-facing upload routes (RFQ attachments, sample attachments, print-area artwork, BOM imports, xlsm import, backup restore, machine-technical import, importWizard) — too much churn for D-42 to go-live (2026-07-30). Acceptance: bump + lockfile diff + per-route DMG smoke + `npm audit --omit=dev` returns 0 high. CI "Vulnerability scan" job remains red as carried-forward baseline (acknowledged at memory `project_golive.md` since the 2026-06-18 entry for Sprint B1+B2); admin-merge culture preserved because every PR introduces ZERO new dep regressions. This PR ships the composition surface as an OPT-IN tab while keeping the 3 originals intact for muscle memory + F1 deep-links; Phase 2 (post-soak, pre-training) retires the originals; Phase 3 (MES-3-FIX-56, post-go-live) extracts shared Section components + dedupes ~1,600 LOC from Cpx SubProductRow. **Why composition (not extraction) at Phase 1**: all 3 children (`CalcMaterials` / `CalcInks` / `CalcProcesses`) already read std-state via `useCalc()` context — no prop interface to design, render-as-siblings is byte-equivalent to render-in-tab. Mounting all 3 UNCONDITIONALLY in one render avoids the focus-loss / DOM-identity trap (Lesson 18 + Sprint S-PACK-SHIP step 3 R3 — pinpoint identified by Plan-agent audit before code). Zero state / reducer / calcEngine / Cpx changes — revert is a 1-commit affair (delete CalcCombined.jsx + SUB_TABS entry + i18n keys). **Files**: new `client/src/modules/cost/tabs/StandardCalc/CalcCombined.jsx` (67 LOC incl docstring + 3 stacked sections); `StandardCalc.jsx` SUB_TABS gets `{ id: 'combined', label: 'Combined', labelKey: 'pricing.tab.combined', icon: '⊞' }` inserted between Layout and Materials (head of pricing-phase block) + switch case + `labelKey ? t() : label` fallback (opt-in i18n pattern that doesn't force the other 10 tabs to convert); `StandardCalc.css` adds `.sc-combined-section` + heading styles (no inline styles per Lesson 6); `i18n/domains/costing.js` adds 4 keys EN+VI (`pricing.tab.combined` = "Combined" / "Tổng hợp", + 3 `pricing.section.*` headings); `help/content.js` adds `standard-combined` entry with `relatedTabs` pointing to the 3 originals + user guide docx rebuilt via `node scripts/help/build-user-guide.mjs`. **Tests**: 1243/1243 client green (no change from baseline — composition doesn't touch test surface); ESLint 0/0 on touched files; bundle self-check confirms 4 i18n keys + `standard-combined` help entry + `.sc-combined-section` CSS class all shipped in minified dist. **Dev-environment detour** — initial smoke on `:5174` blocked by **MES-3-FIX-57** (seed-admin.js writes to wrong path in dev workspace because it never calls `authService.init(DATA_DIR)`). Pivoted per spec Option C: build SERVER DMG + verify in real topology with `Vicky@1982` admin. **Hardware-verified rc1** SERVER DMG SHA `f5c95114c5a408bcd1e58ee3f481f043343014de6edd576c36caf1ec7431625f` — Henry "smoke test green" → admin-merge squash per Sprint S-D21 culture: R3 focus-loss test passed (Material `usage` / Ink `AREA%` / Process `setup_h` keep focus on keystroke), cross-section live update works (Process workcenter → Ink RUN cost updates without tab switch), section parity verified (alt-materials toggle / MOQ tier override bar / `.sc-input-warn` red borders all render in Combined), cross-tab parity confirmed (Combined ↔ standalone tabs show same values), VI locale labels correct (Tổng hợp / Vật tư / Mực in / Công đoạn), 3 standalone tabs unchanged. **Out of scope** (filed): Phase 2 retirement + Phase 3 extraction (MES-3-FIX-56), seed-admin dev-init fix (MES-3-FIX-57), labelKey conversion for the 10 pre-existing tabs (S-I18N-COVER backlog).

→ which quote? → when submitted? → who quoted? → where in pipeline?" before the identity/financials block. **8 new cells per row**: Sale Owner (adjacent to identity block per PR #157 admin-scan convention), END CU (Lesson 21 fallback `s.end_cu || s.project`), PROJECT (canonical `s.project_name` — NEVER `s.project` per Lesson 21 trap), MATERIALS (bulleted Main.Mat join via shared `collectDrwMaterials`), PRICE USD (relabel of pre-fix "Sell" via `qh.sell_price` key — same data), PRICE VND (RAW `s.selling_price_vnd`, NO USD×rate fallback per spec — intentional divergence from QuoteHistory which DOES fall back, accepted because inbox shows operator-entered VND verbatim for accounting/sales agreement), VA% (`r.va` fraction), CONTR.% (`r.contribution` preferred, falls back to legacy `r.contr_pct / 100` per Sprint 1.7f). **Helper extraction** `client/src/modules/cost/tabs/PendingApprovalsInbox.columns.helpers.js` (~100 LOC) — `deriveInboxRow(q)` returns the bag pinned to QuoteHistory contract; pure, vanilla node:test, 30 tests covering project_name vs project (Lesson 21), end_cu fallback, raw VND no-fallback (C2 test catches accidental USD×rate fallback), pickFiniteNumber guard (D2 test catches `+''` → 0 / `+null` → 0 JS gotcha), legacy contr_pct/gm_pct coerce, Main.Mat filter delegate to `Summarize.materials.helpers`, defensive null/undefined/partial state. **Shared filter infra** — `useQuoteFilters()` + `<ScopedFilterBar>` + `applyQuoteFilters(items, debouncedFilter, item => quoteAccessor(item.q))` mirrors QuoteHistory exactly; filter applied AFTER `PENDING_STATES` + viewMode so "N in my queue" header tag stays anchored to true queue size while "N of M shown" counter reflects filtered subset. Filter state lives outside `useAbortableFetch` so 30s poll + SSE refresh don't reset it. **i18n** — zero new strings; all 8 column labels reuse existing `qh.*` keys (`qh.sale_owner` / `qh.end_cu` / `qh.project` / `qh.materials` / `qh.sell_price` / `qh.price_vnd` / `qh.va_pct` / `qh.contr_pct`); 1 new key `qh.quoted_by` EN+VI parity (`QUOTED BY` / `NGƯỜI BÁO GIÁ`) added for the rename. New helper `fmtVnd` inlined next to `fmtPrice` (5 lines, matches `CalcLeadTimeNotice.helpers.fmtVnd` contract — `'—'` for 0/NaN/non-finite). **Tests**: 1243/1243 client tests green (1213 baseline + 30 new helper). ESLint clean on 3 new/changed files. Bundle self-check via 8 `qh.*` keys + `PendingApprovalsInbox` identifier in minified dist. **Hardware-verified rc3** SERVER DMG SHA `793287df0ded138613ca790631ab764852c07e36c511583a21c764980fd10166` — Henry "Verify green" → admin-merge squash per Sprint S-D21 culture. **Out of scope** (filed for future if operators ask): per-tier price in inbox (active-tier only matches existing "Sell" column semantic), sort by new columns (FIFO `submittedAt` preserved per spec — separate ticket if reviewer demand surfaces).

> **MES-3-FIX-55 — Desktop installer scripts rebuild client bundle as prereq shipped 2026-06-17 (SHA: `d5aeedb` via PR #159).** Surfaced earlier today during PR #157 hardware verify: rc2 DMG (`07bfca25…`) was byte-different from rc1 but the Sale Owner column reorder didn't appear on verify because `client/dist/assets/Summarize-*.js` mtime stuck at 08:20 (rc1 baseline). Root cause: `scripts/build-mac-installers.mjs` + `scripts/build-windows-installers.mjs` only ran electron-builder, which packages `client/dist` via `extraResources` — no step triggered `vite build`. Silent trap: build succeeds, DMG is byte-different (different role marker, different zip-metadata timestamps), self-checks pass — but UI changes don't ship. Burned one full verify cycle before manual `cd client && npm run build` → rc3 fixed it. **Fix**: inject "Prebuild client bundle" step at top of `main()` in both installer scripts; runs `npm --prefix client run build` via existing `spawnP` helper, bails on non-zero exit. Skippable via `OPS_SKIP_CLIENT_REBUILD=1` env var mirroring the existing `OPS_SKIP_NATIVE_OVERLAY_CHECK` emergency-override pattern (Lesson 28 / MES-3-FIX-19). Order: client rebuild → native overlay check → electron-builder (client first because UI is most-likely-changed input; fail-fast on Vite errors before slower steps). Mac + Windows symmetry preserved even though Win build is paused per S-RELEASE-DAY1A Q3a (Win DMG stays v1.5.12 until S-WIN-PORT). **Tests**: no automated coverage — build scripts have no test surface. Smoke-tested by running `npm run build:server:mac` on branch — log shows new "Prebuild: rebuilding client bundle (vite build)..." line, vite built in 463ms, fresh bundle hash (`index-aYtRlyqM.js`) shipped vs prior (`index-BuhB72Rq.js`). Admin-merged via squash. **Out of scope**: CI gate that fails the desktop build if `client/dist` is missing or older than any source file (would require mtime comparison across 3000+ files; smoke-test + the eyes-on bundle-hash check in PR descriptions is sufficient for this 1-operator release model).
>
> **Hotfix MES-3-FIX-54 — Login change-pwd silently fails for TOTP users (P0 go-live blocker) shipped 2026-06-17 (SHA: `bac8437` via PR #158).** Henry hardware report mid-Sale-Owner verify cycle: "đổi pass ở ngoài màn login nhưng không được" (Session Expired modal, Remember-me checked, clicked Change password link, filled fields, Sign in succeeded BUT next login still required old password — no visible error). **Root cause** (Lesson 7 cross-verified — security-sensitive code, mandatory grep): `LoginPage.handleLogin` posted `/api/auth/change-pwd` immediately after `await login()` regardless of `loginRes.totp_required`. For TOTP users, login() returns `{success:false, totp_required:true}` with a pre-TOTP session token; the change-pwd POST hits server with that token; `getSessionUser()` returns null because `if (!s.totp_verified) return null` at `server/services/authService.js` → 401. Client throws "Change password failed" + setError, but UI has already swapped to TOTP screen (LoginPage line ~566 branch) which renders `totpError`, NOT `error` from login form. **Failure invisible**, user completes TOTP, enters app, password silently unchanged. **Secondary bug**: hand-rolled fetch in pre-fix code read `localStorage.getItem('ops_token')` only — when Remember-me unchecked, `setToken` writes to sessionStorage (`api.js:65`) so the hand-rolled fetch sees null token → 401 even in non-TOTP path. Existing `costApi.changePwd` helper (`api.js:504`) reads both stores via `getToken()` (`api.js:34-42`) but was unused. **Fix (Approach A — defer-until-totp, server gate untouched)**: new pure helper `client/src/components/Auth/loginChangePwdDispatcher.js` (~60 LOC) with `decideChangePwdDispatch({changeMode, oldPwd, newPwd}, loginResult)` returning `'noop'` / `'change-now'` / `'defer-until-totp'` + `shouldDispatchAfterTotp(pending, verifyResult)` post-gate; defensive contract — defer takes precedence over success when both flags set (the SAFER path because a deferred POST is recoverable; a pre-TOTP POST silently 401s). `LoginPage.handleLogin` consults dispatcher: defer → stash `pendingPwdChange` in React state (NOT localStorage/sessionStorage — new pwd never persisted); change-now → dispatch via `costApi.changePwd` immediately (replaces hand-rolled fetch — secondary bug fix). `LoginPage.runVerifyTOTP` (after verifyTOTP success) checks pending + dispatches; error surfaces inline on TOTP screen via `setTotpError` instead of silent swallow (root cause of "invisible failure" symptom). `wrappedCancelTotp` clears pending so leaving TOTP doesn't silently apply old pending on next login (correctness — wrong account, wrong rotation). `wrappedCompleteTotpEnrollment` covers rare first-login + must_change_password + enrollment-required combo (dispatches after enrollment completes; errors via Toast since enrollment UI lacks error sink — operator can retry via Settings → Change password if it fails post-enroll). 3 new i18n keys EN+VI parity: `login.change.post_totp_failed` / `post_enroll_ok` / `post_enroll_failed`. **Server-side `/auth/change-pwd` TOTP gate UNCHANGED** — Approach C (relax server to accept pre-TOTP session) explicitly rejected per ticket spec to avoid weakening security (attacker with stolen pwd could rotate before TOTP cleanup, hides pivot from audit). **Tests**: 14 vanilla node:test in `loginChangePwdDispatcher.test.js` mapping 1:1 to spec items — A1 noop changeMode-off, A2 defer TOTP user (Spec test 1), A3 change-now non-TOTP (Spec test 2), A4 must_change_password+TOTP (Spec test 6), A5-A10 edge cases (null/undefined/conflict/precedence/no-trim/defensive undef-input), B1-B4 shouldDispatchAfterTotp gate. 1213/1213 client tests green (1199 baseline + 14 new). ESLint 0 errors / 1 pre-existing inline-style warning unchanged (Lesson 6 deferred). **Hardware-verified DMG** (SERVER 1.6.0 SHA-256 `ec657511bcd6342718a4fc050ec83e766b60cfbd017eee3b2b62edd77f885700`) — bundle self-check via i18n strings (`post_totp_failed` + `post_enroll_failed` present in minified `index-BuhB72Rq.js`); Henry "verify green đã merged" → admin-merge squash per Sprint S-D21 culture. **Out of scope**: integration test for the AuthContext+LoginPage React flow (repo uses vanilla node:test, no React testing infra — coverage gap acceptable for hotfix scope; helper unit tests + hardware verify cover the contract).

''` (additive — no migration, no factory change, legacy quotes without the field render empty cell). **Quote History**: added `'sale'` to `QUOTE_HISTORY_COLUMN_KEYS` between `owner` and `direct_cu` (column key `'sale'` not `'sale_owner'` to match the existing `'owner'` brevity convention); render position mirrors `QUOTE_HISTORY_COLUMNS` array via config-driven `visibleColumns.map` (S-D20-COLS-TOGGLE-PHASE-2 pattern — no hand-written `<td>`); column flagged `sortable: true` with `sale: (q) => (q.state?.sale_owner || '').toLowerCase()` sort fn (Lesson 21 falsy-safe — `q.state` undefined on partially-saved quote never throws). **i18n**: 2 new keys in `domains/sales.js` — `qh.sale_owner` EN `'SALE OWNER'` / VI `'NV BÁN HÀNG'`. **Tests**: `QuoteHistory.columns.test.js` column-count 26 → 27, visibility default-hidden subtract 21 → 22, expected sortable-cols set adds `'sale'`, new `sale` sort fn test (empty / value / falsy state defense). 1198/1198 client tests green; ESLint clean. **Pre-merge audit Path 1 chosen** (smoke on `:5173` dev surface instead of full DMG cycle per Henry's `"Đừng đốt một chu kỳ DMG cho một cột"`): Part A (lint identical 332/4/328 between main and PR; `check:deps` exit 0; zero new imports/packages), Part B (6 meaningful CI checks green), Part C (10 sub-items — column order, sort fn empty-safe, stale-key drop compatibility, accessor reads correct field, render position, colSpan dynamic, CSV no double-add, i18n EN+VI, no inline styles, additive diff) — all clean. **Out of scope**: per-Sale-Owner filter dropdown (sort-only via header click sufficient for v1.6 operator workflow), inline edit on Quote History row (read-only attribution column — write happens upstream in Pricing tab), Sale Owner autocomplete from prior quotes (deferred unless operators report repetitive typing).

> **Hotfix MES-3-FIX-53 — Cpx tier MOQ/EAU/Price wrote to wrong state slice shipped 2026-06-16 (SHA: `6f3e0e8` via PR #155).** P0 silent-data-loss surfaced during Sprint S-PACK-SHIP-PER-TIER rc20 hardware verify when Henry hit Cpx Pricing tab tier > 0 and the Price USD ↔ VND auto-convert didn't fire (MOQ1 + all of Std worked fine). Lesson-7 cross-validated via fresh `git worktree add main` worktree: blame points to `449099d7` (PR #41 Sprint S-ALT-MAT, 2026-05-11) — pre-existing 36 days, NOT introduced by pack-ship. **Root cause**: `ComplexCalc.jsx:1396` `updateExtraMoqWithRate` dispatched `SET_EXTRA_MOQ` for every Cpx tier input; the `SET_EXTRA_MOQ` reducer case (`calcReducer.js:516-520`) writes ONLY to `state.stdState.extra_moqs` — wrong slice for Cpx. Visible symptom = VND mirror blank (binding reads `cplxState.extra_moqs[i].price_vnd` which never updated). Hidden severity = every Cpx tier keystroke landed in stdState slice → **data loss on save round-trip** for tier > 0 price + EAU + price_vnd + target + target_vnd. DecimalInput's local string mirror masked the bug visually during the focused-field session, so the issue went undetected until the pack-ship sprint's tier-focused workflow surfaced it. **Fix** (2 surgical edits, pattern mirrors `SET_STD/CPLX_TIER_PACKING_FIELD` split from Sprint S-PACK-SHIP-PER-TIER step 2): new reducer action `SET_CPLX_EXTRA_MOQ` — identical payload shape `{idx, field, value}` but targets `cplxState.extra_moqs` (clone-not-mutate: array new + element new). `ComplexCalc.jsx:1396` flipped from `SET_EXTRA_MOQ` → `SET_CPLX_EXTRA_MOQ`. Std's `SET_EXTRA_MOQ` contract untouched — 5 Std callsites in `CalcHeader.jsx` + `CalcMaterials.jsx` + `CalcProcesses.jsx` continue to write to stdState. USD ↔ VND mirror logic inside `updateExtraMoqWithRate` unchanged; the patch now lands in cplxState so the VND field actually re-renders. **Tests**: 12 red→green in new `client/src/context/calcReducer.cplxExtraMoq.test.js` covering slice routing (cplxState updated, stdState untouched), 6-field coverage (moq/eau/price/price_vnd/target/target_vnd), Std no-regress, immutability across 4 levels, legacy heal (cplxState without extra_moqs), and the severity-justifying **DATA-LOSS round-trip** test — operator types tier 1 price=0.2 + eau=80000 → `JSON.stringify(cplxState) → JSON.parse` → assert values survive on cplxState slice; this test fails RED pre-fix, GREEN post-fix, providing concrete proof of the data-loss path rather than speculation. 1198/1198 client tests green (1186 baseline + 12 new); golden 128/128 untouched; Std tier no-regress confirmed. **Hardware-verified rc22** (SERVER DMG 174 MB, built from hotfix branch with bundle self-check confirming both `SET_CPLX_EXTRA_MOQ` and `SET_STD_TIER_PACKING_FIELD` shipped together) — Henry "verify green" → admin-merge squash per Sprint S-D21 culture. **Data-integrity follow-up — CLOSED 2026-06-16 (no affected data)**: Henry confirmed scope review post-fix. The risk window 2026-05-11 → 2026-06-16 contains zero saved Cpx multi-tier quotes (≥2 tier, with price entered at tier > 0) — pre-go-live (CCL Vietnam Hai Duong D-0 = 2026-07-21), all activity test-only, 7 production operators not yet on system. Std + Cpx single-tier never hit the buggy code path. No audit / back-fill / remediation script needed; forward-fix via #155 is sufficient.

> **Sprint S-PACK-SHIP-PER-TIER — Per-MOQ Packing & Shipping cost (Std + Cpx) shipped 2026-06-16** (4-commit vertical slice — read-path → reducer → UI → exporter — additive heal-on-read, no schema bump). **Step 1 (SHA: `2fde58b`)** calcEngine read-path: `buildTierState` (line 1226) appends `if (em.packing) Object.assign(base, em.packing)` so `buildStdRowsPayload` / `buildCpxRowsPayload` (line 2448) loop tiers via buildTierState pick up the override that pre-sprint only landed in `getActiveTierState` (line 1331). `aggregateComplex` (line 2305-2323) threads `tierIdx` into `parentPsSt = { ...cs, ...tierPacking, moq: activeMoq }` so Cpx parent pack/ship reflects per-tier override. 8 red→green tests in `calcEngine.packingTier.test.js` including ship_qty-pinned hardening that catches false-positives from `calcShipping`'s `ship_qty || moq` divisor divergence. **Step 2 (SHA: `f155da4`)** reducer: new `SET_STD_TIER_PACKING_FIELD` + `SET_CPLX_TIER_PACKING_FIELD` actions in `calcReducer.js`, route by `active_moq_idx` — tier 0 writes top-level `state[field]` (mirrors `SET_STD_FIELD` / `SET_CPLX_FIELD`), tier>0 clones `extra_moqs[idx-1].packing` and either `delete packing[field]` (when `value === '' || value == null`) or `packing[field] = value` (including explicit 0). Henry's rule encoded in reducer (not UI helper) so the dễ-vỡ explicit-0 case is testable at the reducer layer. Clone-not-mutate at 5 levels (state / slice / extra_moqs / em / packing). 12 red→green tests covering Std + Cpx mirror — tier-0 route, tier-1 isolation (no spillover to tier 2 or top-level), empty-string + null deletion, explicit-0 preservation, immutability at every level, create-packing-on-empty-tier. **Step 3 (SHA: `51d7a92`)** UI: `CalcPackingShip.jsx` (Std) + Packing tab in `ComplexCalc.jsx` (Cpx) rewritten to bind via `resolveTierField(em, st, field)` from new `services/packingTierField.js` — uses `Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(em.packing, field)` NOT `??`/`||` so explicit 0 override surfaces as 0 instead of silent fallback. Visual states (no inline styles per Lesson 6): `.sc-pack-tier-ovr` violet+bold when override active, `.sc-pack-tier-inherit` italic gray on tier>0 inherited, `.sc-pack-reset` ↻ button beside input only when override exists (explicit revert affordance). Hint banner `.sc-pack-tier-hint` at top of tab explains override mode. `DecimalInput.jsx` surgical 1-prop change: new `preserveEmpty` opt-in (default `false` → 100+ callsites byte-identical) flips blur-with-empty from `onChange(0)` to `onChange('')` via new `blurEmptyValue(preserveEmpty)` helper extracted for unit-testability. The 5 DecimalInput-wrapped pack/ship fields (`container_cost` / `box_cost` / `other_packing` / `shipping_cost` / `other_ship`) opt into `preserveEmpty` when `activeIdx > 0` so clearing the field sends `''` to the reducer → deletes the override key → reverts to base. Totals (`Total Packing/pcs` + `Total Shipping/pcs`) compute from active-tier-merged state via `getActiveTierState` (Std) or inline merge (Cpx) so operators see override effect live — pre-fix always showed tier-0 numbers. Module-scope wrappers `PackRow` + `CpxPackRow` per react-hooks/static-components lint. Bonus surgical cleanup: dropped pre-existing unused `toDisplay` import from `DecimalInput.jsx` + stale "re-exported for callers" comment (eslint-disable was on the wrong line of the multi-line import; lint-staged correctly flagged it when the file was staged). 10 red→green tests in `packingTierField.test.js` covering all 7 resolver paths + 3 `blurEmptyValue` cases. **Step 4 (SHA: `37e063b`)** exporter: `sheets/07-pack-ship.js` accepts `tierIdx` (default 0 BC) and merges `state.extra_moqs[tierIdx-1].packing` over base state before writing the K/V table. `index.js` passes `tierIdx` into `buildPackShipSheet` alongside the other tier-aware sheets (mirrors PR #58 for sheets 03/04/05/08). Three state factories (`createStdState`, `createEmptyStdState`, `createCplxState` at `calcEngine.js`) seed `options: ''` — additive heal-on-read so legacy quotes that lack the field read it as empty string via `get('options') ?? ''` rather than triggering a controlled-component warning. **Quote History column repurposed**: column key `ver` → `option`, labelKey `qh.ver` → `qh.option` (EN: `OPTION` / VI: `TÙY CHỌN`). Render swaps the `q.version`-derived badge for `state.options` string with ellipsis + native `title` tooltip. Kept STD/CPX type badge + MaterialActiveBadge (orthogonal row-meta indicators). Adds sort fn (`state.options` lowercased) so the column is sortable. `resolveSortKey` rewrites legacy `'ver'` → `'option'` so saved sort prefs from v1.6.0-rc6 keep working (same pattern as `'npi'` → `'owner'` from PR #122). Dropped unused `verN` / `verCls` from `ctx.derived` + orphan CSS classes `.qh-vb` / `.v-grn` / `.v-blu` / `.v-pur` (no other references). Tests: expected sortable-cols set bumped 13 → 14; added 3 cases (option sort fn empty / value, `'ver'` → `'option'` rewrite + hidden fallback). 1153/1153 pass. **PR #141 (S-OPTIONS-LAYOUT, SHA `2de82df`)**: Henry verified rc8 hardware and asked to move Options inline with the other 3 RFQ & Certification fields (originally a full-width textarea on row 2). Dropped `sc-field-span2` from RFQ Number, dropped `sc-field-full` from Options, inserted Options between RFQ Number and Request UL — all 4 fields now share a single row of `sc-grid4` (1 col each). Textarea height drops to `rows=1` + `minHeight: 2.2em` to match the adjacent input/select but keeps `resize: vertical` for longer notes. **Out of scope**: per-MOQ-tier Options (single quote-level field is sufficient for operator notes), Options autocomplete from prior quotes (deferred unless operators report repetitive typing), search filter in Quote History keyed to Option (sort-only via column header click). > **Sprint S-INPUT-WARN — Red-border warnings for missing AREA % + EAU shipped 2026-06-12 (SHA: `3a5519c` via PR #139).** Two operator traps Henry hit during today's pre-go-live hardware verify both produced silently-wrong cost numbers when a required input was left blank, with no visual cue pointing at the field to fix. Both now flip to the existing `.sc-input-warn` class (red `#ef4444` border `!important`) when the missing input would zero a meaningful downstream cost. **Inks tab — AREA %**: `run_s = price × qpa_lm × area_pct × width_m / coverage / scrap` — when `area_pct = 0` the whole numerator collapses and the RUN column shows "—" with no hint. Cell now flips red when the ink row is "real" (color, ifs_code, or print_type set — same identity gate `calcInk` uses internally at `calcEngine.js:480`) but `area_pct` stays 0. Native title tooltip "AREA % bắt buộc để tính giá mực RUN". Applied to both Std (`CalcInks.jsx:325`) and Cpx (`SubProductRow.jsx:1219`). **MOQ tier — EAU**: `calcProcess` computes the tooling EAU cap as `st.annual_qty × product_lifetime` (`calcEngine.js:639`); when `annual_qty = 0` it falls back to `MOQ × 1`, silently inflating per-unit tooling cost. This was Henry's "tools price = $237.50" report this morning that turned out to be missing EAU — `do tôi chưa nhập EAU`. Cell now flips red when at least one process has `tool_cost > 0` but `annual_qty = 0`. Native title tooltip "EAU bắt buộc để tính giá khuôn (Tooling) đúng". Applied to both Std (`CalcHeader.jsx`) and Cpx (`ComplexCalc.jsx`). Computed via memoised `(processes||[]).some(p => Number(p?.tool_cost) > 0) && !(Number(annual_qty) > 0)`. **CSS scope**: reuses existing `.sc-input-warn` rule at `StandardCalc.css:474` (red `#ef4444` border with `!important` so it overrides `cc-det-inp` + `sc-moq-inp`); Cpx imports `StandardCalc.css` already at `ComplexCalc.jsx:47` + `SubProductRow.jsx:54` so no new CSS needed. **Tests**: 1153/1153 client tests pass; ESLint 0 errors, 19 pre-existing inline-style warnings unchanged. Henry signed off + asked for rc7 DMG build immediately after merge. **Out of scope**: warnings on other manual-entry fields that can silently zero costs (Material `usage`, Process `setup_h`, etc.) — deferred unless operators report further silent-zero confusion; `design_process` already has `sc-input-warn` so this is just continuing the established pattern (`RfqInfoCard.jsx:244`).

> **Sprint S-NPI-PARTS — Read-only NPI Parts List viewer shipped 2026-06-11 (SHA: `2a77660` via PR #136).** v1.6 Option C — Henry-confirmed read-only scope (edit/save deferred), new tab under Quoting & Pricing section. New `client/src/modules/cost/tabs/NpiPartsList.{jsx,css}` (427 LOC + 247 CSS) + `scripts/build-npi-parts-snapshot.mjs` xlsx → JSON exporter (130 LOC). Source: `2. TEMPLATES/Cost Ref data/NPI/NPI Quote part list.xlsx` (~25k rows × 64 cols, Henry's production reference data). Snapshot path: `client/public/npi-parts/parts-snapshot.json` (~30 MB) — path picked specifically to AVOID Vite's `/data` proxy that routes to Express :3000 (first attempt at `client/public/data/npi-parts.json` returned 401 because Vite proxied the static asset request to the Express auth-protected `/data` route). Express `compression` middleware reduces wire to ~1 MB gzip; DMG bzip2 layer keeps installer growth ~1 MB. **Excel serial dates** (RFQ date / Quoted date / Week) converted to ISO YYYY-MM-DD via classic 25569-subtraction (Lotus-1-2-3 1900 leap-year bug baked in); fully-null rows dropped; cell whitespace trimmed; header keys trimmed (xlsx had leading/trailing space in some headers). Payload shape: `{generated_at, source_file, source_mtime, source_size_bytes, row_count, columns, rows}` so viewer can validate snapshot age + drift-detect on rebuild. **Viewer wiring**: lazy chunk via `React.lazy` + `Suspense` in `CostModule.jsx` (`TAB_COMPONENTS['npi-parts-list']` + `TAB_TITLES`); `sectionDefs.js` entry under `quoting` after `quote-history`; new sidebar `parts_list` icon (rect + 3 lines ledger SVG) in `SidebarIcon.jsx`. **Features**: search box (6 SEARCHABLE_KEYS — Part Name / Code IFS / System code / Customer / PIC / Direct Project), year filter (RFQ-date YYYY extraction via `rowYear`), `<ColumnsToggle>` reuse from Sprint S-D20-COLS-TOGGLE-PHASE-1 (PR #120) with 52 default-hidden keys opt-in (`STORAGE_KEY = 'ops-cost-npi-parts-cols'`), client-side pagination `PER_PAGE = 200` (mirrors MaterialLibrary pattern), double-click row → showcard modal (`Modal.Header` / `Modal.Body` / `Modal.Footer` subcomponents) with 9 SHOWCARD_FIELDS (RFQ date, Quoted date, System code, Code IFS, Unit price VND/USD, Customer, MOQ, Process) + 5 TOOLING_KEYS (woodie / Pinacle die / Rotary Die / Dieset / NC die). `fmtVnd` en-US 0 decimals, `fmtUsd` 4 decimals, `fmtInt` 0 decimals — `font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums` everywhere. **i18n**: 14 new keys under `npi_parts.*` (title / row_count / readonly_notice / search_placeholder / year_filter_label / year_all / columns_title / no_match / prev / next / page_x_of_y / snapshot_generated_at / showcard_title / showcard_tooling_fee / load_error_title / load_error_hint); `nav.tab.npi_parts_list` for sidebar label. `.gitignore` excludes the 30 MB snapshot — operator rebuilds via `npm run build:npi-parts` before DMG cut. Henry verified rc6 DMG green ("verify green, go next step"). **Out of scope**: edit/save (Option C readonly per Q1); server-side pagination (25k rows fits in browser memory comfortably with `font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums` + `max-width: 240px ellipsis` cells); full-text search across all 64 cols (6-key indexed search sufficient per operator workflow); useNpiMaterials hook (Henry referenced but doesn't exist — MaterialLibrary uses inline state, not a custom hook).

Explore agent audit found 49 hardcoded English strings across Sidebar nav + Settings tabs + Planning module + footer. **Patched 33 P0+P1 holes**: `Sidebar.jsx` Design Tools + Machine Technical changed `label:` → `labelKey:` (existing i18n entries finally wired); `sectionDefs.js` PLANNING_SECTIONS retrofitted — all 6 tab labels (Order Entry / BOM Explosion / Material Check / Capacity Planning / Work Orders / WIP Tracker) + 3 section headers (Production / Scheduling / Tracking) → labelKey; footer `(me)` + `Active now` → `t()` calls via new `nav.footer.me_tag` + `nav.footer.active_now`. `Settings.jsx` MENU_SECTIONS items + **section headers** now resolve via `i18nKey ? t(i18nKey) : section.label` — was previously resolving items but ignoring section labels (root cause of the VI screenshot regression). `ProfileSection` added `useI18n()` hook + 9 form label keys (`settings.profile.*`: upload_hint / full_name_vn / english_name / email / phone / username / id_no / save_btn / about_title) in `domains/basis.js`. **String additions**: `nav.tab.design_tools`, `nav.tab.machine_technical`, 6 planning tab keys, 3 planning section keys, 14 npi_parts.\* keys (companion to S-NPI-PARTS), `settings.item.about` + `settings.item.import_legacy`. **Bug fixes also rolled in**: `nav.tab.metrics` corrected to "Số liệu Quản trị", `nav.tab.audit_log` corrected to "Nhật ký Kiểm toán", `nav.team_online_title` corrected to "Đội ngũ đang online" (previously wrong/missing VI). **Deferred 16 P2 holes**: DDL editor / Audit Log internal / Metrics / Kiosk Admin VI translations — admin-only surfaces, lower operator impact, accepted as post-go-live polish. > **Sprint S-WIZARD-CORS — Desktop setup wizard CORS + firstRunCompleted defensive guard shipped 2026-06-11 (SHA: `e39bb63` via PR #133).** Two bugs from the Day 2 hardware verify cascade, bundled as 2 commits squashed. **Bug A (CORS)**: Henry diagnosed correctly via independent analysis ("xác nhận chính xác 100% chẩn đoán CORS"). Wizard rendered at `data:text/html` URL has null origin; renderer `fetch()` to `http://10.102.3.61:3100/health` is cross-origin; embedded Express returns no `Access-Control-Allow-Origin` header → blocked silently with "Failed to fetch". Safari worked because direct user navigation doesn't trigger CORS; the Electron renderer's same-origin policy flagged it. **Fix (Option 2 Electron-clean per Henry's preference)**: replace renderer `fetch()` with `/__probe__?url=<encoded>` sentinel URL intercepted in main process via `webRequest.onBeforeRequest` (extends existing `/__firstrun__` sentinel pattern). Handler calls `probeServer(url)` at new `desktop/utils/netProbe.js` (server-side `node:http` request, no CORS layer involved), then `executeJavaScript('window.__probeResolve(...)')` pushes JSON result back to the wizard renderer. Server-side CORS policy untouched (cleaner than relaxing prod-server CORS for a one-off wizard probe). **Bug B (firstRunCompleted defensive guard)**: Henry's debug log surfaced `ERR_INVALID_URL` at boot — root cause: user closed wizard without saving → app set `firstRunCompleted=true` unconditionally → next boot `loadURL('')` since `remoteUrl` still empty → bricked app needing manual `~/Library/Application Support/.../config.json` edit. **Two-layer fix at `desktop/main.js`**: (1) **Recovery** — boot-time detect `BUILD_ROLE==='client' && firstRunCompleted===true && mode==='thin' && remoteUrl===''` resets the flag to re-trigger the wizard; (2) **Prevention** — only set `firstRunCompleted=true` if `mode==='embedded' || (mode==='thin' && remoteUrl.length>0)` (the bootable states). Closes the wizard-close-without-save → bricked-app failure mode. Tests: license + manifest unchanged (desktop wrapper, no test surface for IPC sentinel). Henry hardware-verified on rc4/rc5 DMG → admin-merge per Sprint S-D21 culture. **SUPERSEDED 2026-09-10 by S-FIRSTRUN-IPC (`d5ed84a`, PR #279)** — the `/__probe__` sentinel described above never worked from the dialog's opaque `data:` origin and has been replaced by `ipcRenderer.invoke`. Read this entry as history, not as a description of the code.
>
> **Sprint S-DESKTOP-HMAC — Desktop emergency fixes #1 + #2 from Day 2 hardware verify shipped 2026-06-11 (SHA: `b720feb` via PR #132, 2 commits squashed).** **Bug #1 (OPS_EXPORT_HMAC_KEY missing on first run)**: Henry installed CLIENT DMG, created admin account, login failed. Root cause: embedded Express requires `OPS_EXPORT_HMAC_KEY` to start; first-run CLIENT DMG didn't auto-set it; embedded server refused to boot, login route returned 503. **Fix**: auto-generate 64-hex via `crypto.randomBytes(32).toString('hex')` at `desktop/main.js`, mirroring existing TOTP + KIOSK key gen pattern. Persist to `electron-store` (`exportHmacKey` key) so key survives reinstalls AND remains stable across DMG upgrades (re-exports of old xlsx audits stay verifiable). Pattern: `if (!process.env.OPS_EXPORT_HMAC_KEY)` → load from store → if missing or `length !== 64`, gen new + `store.set` + `log.info('[main] Generated new OPS_EXPORT_HMAC_KEY')` → `process.env.OPS_EXPORT_HMAC_KEY = hmacKey`. **Bug #2 (CLIENT dialog flips topology to EMBEDDED)**: Henry's CLIENT DMG showed "EMBEDDED · LOCALHOST" mode banner after the loadURL-fail recovery dialog defaulted to "Reset về Embedded + Restart" — silently switched a CLIENT install into an EMBEDDED install (creates a phantom local server, breaks the intended thin-client topology, hides real connectivity problems from operator). **Fix**: branch dialog buttons on `BUILD_ROLE` at `desktop/main.js` — CLIENT gets "Chạy lại setup wizard" (clears `remoteUrl` only, keeps `mode='thin'`, opens wizard on next boot); SERVER keeps "Reset về Embedded" (legitimate recovery path for embedded build). Both bugs surfaced during Day 2 SERVER+CLIENT DMG hardware verify ahead of training session. Henry hardware-verified on rc2 DMG. Commitlint hiccups along the way (`fix(desktop):` → `fix(apps/desktop):` per closed enum; "CLIENT" uppercase → lowercase per subject-case rule) noted as Lesson candidate for `apps/desktop` scope memorisation.
>

Tolerant helper — malformed snapshot still bumps `source=empty`, never blocks save (mirrors costApi audit() try/catch). Wired into POST /quotes + PATCH /quotes/:id after `upsertQuote` succeeds. 10 new unit tests covering resolveSnapshotSource branches + counter emission + warning aggregation + malformed-input tolerance. **Synthetic benchmark** — `scripts/bench/pricingSnapshot.bench.js` times `freezeLib` + `snapshotPricingParams` + resolver lookup across 100 / 1k / 10k fixtures with realistic state shape (50 mat / 40 wc / 30 coverage rows mirroring production CCL library at v1.5.12). At 10k quotes: `freezeLib` p95 = 38 μs (130× under 5 ms budget), `snapshotPricingParams` p95 = 0 μs, resolver lookup p95 = 0 μs. Confirms snapshot work added zero measurable hot-path cost. Exit 1 on any 10k-fixture p95 breach so CI can opt-in to enforce. **Bilingual EN+VI docs**: `docs/uat/pricing-snapshot-uat.md` — 30-scenario UAT script (20 operator + 10 admin) covering save+freeze, copy quote, legacy heal-on-read, SnapshotPanel UI, Summarize column, xlsx audit sheet, site mismatch detection, library drift shielding, /metrics counter assertions. Executor = Henry solo on Mac DMG. `docs/cutover/PRICING_SNAPSHOT_OPERATOR_GUIDE.md` — operator how-to (badge legend / copy workflow / legacy quote / FAQ). `docs/cutover/PRICING_SNAPSHOT_ADMIN_GUIDE.md` — admin reference (architecture / warnings catalogue / Summarize bulk audit / xlsx review / /metrics signals / library audit guarantee / recovery playbook). **Tests**: server 1265/1265 pass (1 chronic MES-3-FIX-51 SKIP, unrelated). Client 1151/1151 unchanged. Bench exit 0 at all 3 fixture sizes. Admin-merged via squash per Sprint S-D21 culture (CI-red pre-existing per MES-3-FIX-38/50 backlog; green checks all passed). **Out of scope**: Phase 6 stage rollout + production cut-over scheduling (separate sprint S-SNAPSHOT-PHASE-6 with 41-day buffer to go-live D-0 2026-07-21).

Sheet count 10 → 11 across `exportQuote.unit.test.js`. **Summarize "Snapshot" column** — optional, default hidden via `SUMMARIZE_DEFAULT_HIDDEN_KEYS`, renders per-row Frozen/Live/No pill so admin can scan unfrozen quotes at a glance. **CalcCostBreakdown + CplxCostBreakdown** — lifted `snapshotPricingParams` to useMemo + passed through to tier calcAll AND SnapshotPanel so multi-tier displays read uniformly from snapshot. **Tests**: 1039 client + 6 new sheet integration tests (persisted stamps / synthesized labelled / empty labelled / distinct from hidden `_Audit` / surfaces `_warnings` / null snapshot graceful) + 11 helper unit tests. Henry hardware-verified on Mac DMG SERVER 1.5.12 (SHA `2524e376…`) across 4 rebuilds → admin-merged. **Out of scope**: xlsx sheet 11-leadtime (MES-3-FIX-48 deferred), per-quote snapshot diff viewer (future work if auditor demand surfaces).

> **Sprint S-SNAPSHOT-PHASE-3 — Snapshot capture + render wiring shipped 2026-06-10 (SHA: `4ae6931` via PR #128).** Writer + reader sides land. **Writer**: `StandardCalc.jsx` + `ComplexCalc.jsx` `buildQuoteData` calls `freezeLib(lib, state, { userId: user?.id })` AND recomputes `calcAll` + rowsPayload with the resulting snapshot before POST. `useAuth()` threads `user.id` into the freeze options — `_captured_by` previously left null because `freezeLib` had no auth context (hook-only path: `Auth` accessible only inside React tree, NOT from the pure helper). Explicit-option pattern chosen over implicit globals so node:test fixtures stay pure. **Cpx** re-runs `aggregateComplex` post-freeze so each SP's per-tier result inherits the snapshot resolver. **Copy quote bug** (latent since Sprint 14 right-click Copy ship): `pendingQuote.action` was set by Quote History but never READ by either StandardCalc nor ComplexCalc useEffect → copies always treated as plain reload, snapshot kept the ORIGINAL captured_at/by. Fix: useEffect destructures `action` + passes through `loadQuote(quoteType, qState, id, version, action)` 5-positional signature (default `'load'` for BC). Reducer `LOAD_QUOTE` branches on action: when `'copy'`, calls `copySnapshot()` which flips `_synthesized: true` + clears `_captured_at`/`_captured_by` + resets `activeQuoteId` (UI knows this is a draft, not a re-open). **Reader**: `CalcCostBreakdown.jsx` + `CplxCostBreakdown.jsx` resolve via `snapshotPricingParams(state, lib)` once per render in useMemo, passes `{snapshot, source}` to every tier's calcAll call. **Tests**: 998 client + 6 new copy-action reducer cases. Henry hardware-verified on Mac DMG. **Out of scope**: SnapshotPanel UI (deferred to Phase 4), Summarize column (Phase 4), xlsx sheet (Phase 4).

> **Sprint S-SNAPSHOT-PHASE-2 — CalcEngine snapshot reader shipped 2026-06-10 (SHA: `cb3fa2a` via PR #127).** Reader layer + 5th positional options arg. New `createResolver(snapshot, lib)` in `client/src/services/calcEngine.js` returns `{ getMat, getRate, getCoverage }` — when snapshot has a row, return it; otherwise fall back to `lib.*` (synthesized path). Extended 4 hot-path functions with `options = {}` 5th arg: `calcAll(st, allSpResults, lib, subproducts, options)`, `calcInk(state, ink, lib, st, options)`, `calcProcess(...)`, `calcRowBreakdown(...)`, plus `buildStdRowsPayload` / `buildCpxRowsPayload` / `aggregateComplex`. Resolver chosen at function entry: `options.snapshot ? createResolver(snapshot, lib) : { getMat: code => getMatByCode(lib.mat, code), … }`. 11 internal lookups swapped — line 439 coverage, line 536+540 getRateByWC, all materials_main / materials_alt iterators. **Site mismatch detection** — `siteWarnings` array (new name to avoid collision with existing `warnings` from scrap_pct array) populates when `snapshot._site && state.site && snapshot._site !== state.site`, joined into `result._warnings` field at end of calcAll. **BC critical**: 11 existing calcAll callsites preserved — 5th arg defaults `{}` → resolver path `else` branch reads from lib exactly as before. Golden 128/128 test suite passes verbatim. **Tests**: 957 client baseline + 14 new resolver + warning tests + per-tier snapshot independence (active vs non-active tier use the same snapshot but get distinct results via `getActiveTierState`). **Out of scope**: writer-side freezeLib invocation (Phase 3), UI surface (Phase 4).
>
> **Sprint S-SNAPSHOT-PHASE-1 — Pricing snapshot foundation shipped 2026-06-10 (SHA: `296da8b` via PR #126).** Schema + helpers foundation. New `client/src/services/pricingSnapshot.js` with 7 pure helpers: `createEmptySnapshot()` (canonical shape — `{_captured_at, _captured_by, _synthesized, _lib_version, _site, materials, coverage, rates}`), `freezeLib(lib, state, options)` (captures ROWS ACTUALLY USED — walks `state.materials_main` + `materials_alt` + `subproducts[*].materials` for codes, walks processes for workcenters, deep-clones `lib.ddl.coverage` via structuredClone since the table is small), `snapshotPricingParams(state, lib)` (3-branch resolver: persisted snapshot wins → synthesized from lib → empty fallback), and 4 accessor helpers (`getMatFromSnapshot` / `getRateFromSnapshot` / `getCoverageFromSnapshot` / `getSnapshotSite`) shaped to mirror existing `calcEngine.getMatByCode` / `getRateByWC` so Phase 2 wiring stays a 1-line swap. **Heal-on-read migration** — `stdMigration.js` + `cplxMigration.js` add `healPricingSnapshot(state)` idempotent helper invoked in short-circuit + post-migration paths, NO schema version bump (additive-only field, per Sprint S-D21-LEADTIME `safeLeadTime` + PR #110 precedent). 3 pre-existing migration tests asserting ref-equality for fully-current state needed `pricing_snapshot: {}` added to fixtures (heal returns same ref when snapshot already present). **`createStdState` + `createEmptyStdState` + `createCplxState`** factories now stamp `pricing_snapshot: createEmptySnapshot()` at construction so legacy + new quotes always carry the key. **Site-aware audit** confirmed at design time — `lib.rate` arriving here is ALREADY pre-filtered by `activeSite` at `CostLibContext.jsx`, so freezeLib reads `lib.rate` direct (same shape calcEngine has always seen); `state.site` captured as `_site` audit metadata enables Phase 2 mismatch detection. **Tests**: 957 client + 36 new pricingSnapshot.{test,roundtrip.test}.js cases covering empty-shape contract, freeze captures only used rows, persisted-wins-over-lib resolver branch, synthesized flag propagation, deep-clone independence (mutating snapshot doesn't leak to lib), legacy heal-on-read idempotency. **Architectural commitment** — calcEngine stays client-only (server NEVER recomputes); snapshot is the persisted bridge that lets server-side xlsx (Phase 4) read freeze status without re-running calc. **Out of scope**: calcEngine reader (Phase 2), save-handler wiring (Phase 3), UI + xlsx audit sheet (Phase 4), Phase 5 docs/metrics/bench/UAT.
>
> **Sprint S-D20-SUMMARIZE-SCHEMA-EXTEND — Summarize schema +10 cols + bullet polish shipped 2026-06-10 (SHA: `3ed19cc` via PR #124, 4 commits squashed).** Extends the Cost Breakdown (Summarize) tab from 21 → 32 columns by syncing Pricing (Std/Cpx) sub-tab fields directly into the browseable table so operator can filter / sort / CSV-export across Materials + Lead Time & Notice + tooling-total + per-tier VND data without round-tripping through Quote History → Open quote. **New columns**: `#` (UI-only row index, 1-based VISIBLE position recomputed on filter/sort tick), `Project` (state.project_name — canonical Project, distinct from aliased End Customer at state.project per Lesson 21), `Draw Materials` + `Quote Materials` (Σ materials[i].drw_material / .desc; Cpx walks every subproducts[i].materials; **NO dedupe** per operator's BOM-visibility intent — duplicate Main.Mat across SPs preserved), `Tooling Cost (USD)` (quote-level Σ tool_cost via reused `sumToolingCostStd/Cpx` from Sprint S-D21-LEADTIME), `Price (VND)` (per-tier raw from state.selling_price_vnd / extra_moqs[t-1].price_vnd — Sprint 1.7g mirror pattern, no USD×rate fallback per Henry's confirm), 6 × Lead Time & Notice cells (Material L/T, Sample L/T, PO L/T, Remark, Process, Type of Material — heal-on-read via safeLeadTime). **Relabels**: `Tooling` → `Tooling/pcs` (disambiguate from new absolute-USD column), `Price` → `Price (USD)` (column key `usd_price` UNCHANGED so localStorage `ops-cost-summarize-cols` toggle state persists across upgrade). **Bullet rendering** (5 cells): Draw Materials / Quote Materials / Remark / Process / Type of Material render multi-line via `formatBulletList` + `toBulletFromTextarea` pure helpers — `"- item1\n- item2\n- item3"` format; new `<MultilineCell>` component + `.sum-cell-multiline` CSS class (max-height 120px + 4px thin custom scrollbar + native `title` tooltip for full hover content). **Main.Mat filter** (added 2026-06-10 follow-up): Draw/Quote Materials cols filter `row_type === 'Main.Mat'` via new `client/src/modules/cost/lib/rowTypeNormalize.js` (normalizeRowType strips trailing whitespace + numeric suffix so legacy quotes carrying `"Main.Mat 1"` / `"Main.Mat 2"` / `"Process Mat 5"` classify correctly — operator-typed sequence suffixes from pre-Sprint-S-ALT-MAT era). **CSV polish**: row_idx flagged `csvExclude: true` + filter logic in exportCSV (UI anchor but no meaning in re-sortable CSV row); buildCsv extended with optional `opts.headers` array so CSV header row uses operator-facing column labels — `End Customer` not internal key `project` (S-PROJFIX aliased-text trap). **Helper extractions** (per ColumnsToggle.helpers / DesignSyncPicker.fields pattern): `Summarize.materials.helpers.js` (`collectDrwMaterials`, `collectQuoteMaterials`, `formatBulletList`, `toBulletFromTextarea` — 32 vanilla node:test cases including S-ALT-MAT active-set contract + Main.Mat legacy form + CRLF tolerance + non-string defense), `client/src/modules/cost/lib/rowTypeNormalize.js` (16 tests covering canonical / legacy suffix / outer whitespace / unrelated label pass-through), `fmtVnd` added to CalcLeadTimeNotice.helpers.js cạnh `fmtUsd` (Intl.NumberFormat en-US maximumFractionDigits 0 → `10,450` or `"—"` for 0 / NaN / non-finite; 8 tests). Cell-render contract extended BACKWARD-COMPATIBLE: `column.render(row, ri)` takes precedence over `column.fmt(value)` — existing 21 cols keep `fmt` unchanged. **defaultHiddenKeys**: 6 Lead Time cols hidden on first load (`SUMMARIZE_DEFAULT_HIDDEN_KEYS`) — operator opts in via toggle when forensically tracing lead-time commitments. CSV respects visibility — no force-include for the 6 LT cols. **Tests**: 1039/1039 client green (998 baseline + 41 new across 4 helper files). 0 errors / 5 pre-existing inline-style ESLint warnings on Summarize.jsx (Lesson 6 / Sprint 11 P-1 deferred top-5 offenders, unchanged by this work). Henry hardware-verified on Mac DMG SERVER 1.5.12 across 4 rebuilds (build progression captured the bullet + Main.Mat + Price-VND + CSV-header fixes as separate commits before squash). Admin-merged via squash per Sprint S-D21 culture (CI-red pre-existing per MES-3-FIX-36/37/38 backlog). **Out of scope**: per-column inline edit, Ink columns (separate ticket if needed), Cpx Materials defensive filtering (`rowTypeNormalize.js` placed in `lib/` future-proof for that work), xlsx export of the new 11 cols (sheet 11-leadtime already deferred per MES-3-FIX-48).

> **Sprint S-D20-COLS-TOGGLE-PHASE-2 — Columns toggle for Quote History shipped 2026-06-10 (SHA: `8208c24` via PR #122).** Phase 2 of the columns-toggle rollout — applies the shared `<ColumnsToggle>` popover (Phase 1, PR #120 SHA `3405cd5`) to Quote History's 26-column table. Refactors the hardcoded `<thead>` / `<tbody>` JSX into a single module-scoped `QUOTE_HISTORY_COLUMNS` config array driving both header (sort-header row) and body (cell renders) via `visibleColumns.map`. Column config carries `{ key, labelKey, required, sortable, width, thClass, tdClass, tdStyle, tdStyleFn, tdPropsFn, render(q, ctx) }` — render closes over a per-row `ctx` bag with pre-computed `derived` state (`s, r, gmVal, vaVal, contrVal, target, sizeW, sizeH, design, matStr, verN, verCls`) so each of the ~21 visible cells doesn't re-read `q.state` / `q.result` independently. **6 required columns** (cannot be hidden): `num, date, rfq, status, approve, layout` — anchors the workflow regardless of how aggressive the operator's hide list gets. **5 default-hidden cols (Henry confirmed Phase 2 Q7 option C)**: `ul, ifs, dcu_pn, ecu_pn, target` → 21 visible on first load; subsequent operator choices persist via `localStorage['ops-cost-quote-history-cols']` (stale-key drop + corrupt-JSON fallback inherited from Phase 1 `loadVisibleColumns` helper). **Helper extraction** (`QuoteHistory.columns.js`, 171 LOC) — 15 pure `sortFn`s lifted off the old module-level `SORT_KEYS` object with S-PROJFIX (Lesson 21) / Sprint 1.7f / Sprint 1.7g comments preserved verbatim + `resolveSortKey(currentKey, visibleKeys)` fallback (snaps to `'date'` when sortKey hidden OR no longer in config + transparently rewrites legacy `'npi'` → `'owner'` so saved sort prefs from v1.5.12 keep working — column was renamed during this refactor so column.key == sortKey stays symmetric) + `STATUS_ORDER` constant + `QUOTE_HISTORY_COLUMN_KEYS` / `REQUIRED_KEYS` / `DEFAULT_HIDDEN_KEYS` / `STORAGE_KEY` canonical sets. **Resilience guards**: (1) hide-sorted-column → `useEffect` resolves to `'date desc'` (ESLint warns `react-hooks/set-state-in-effect` same as pre-existing `setPage(0)` on filter change at line 462; MES-3-FIX-20/38 backlog culture); (2) 30s polling + SSE refresh do not touch `visibleColumns` state (lives outside `useAbortableFetch` tick — operator never loses column visibility mid-session); (3) empty-state `colSpan` now dynamic (`visibleColumns.length`) so "No quotes found" still spans the full visible table after any hide; (4) `<tr onContextMenu={...}>` wrapper kept at row level — context menu survives the inner `<td>` migration. **Tests**: 984/984 client green (948 baseline + 36 new helper tests) covering every sortFn + S-PROJFIX fallback (`end_cu || project`) + Lesson 21 aliasMap + Sprint 1.7g VND derive + legacy `contr_pct` coerce + status workflow ordinal + `resolveSortKey` all paths + `npi → owner` rewrite + default-hidden Option C set membership. ESLint 0 errors / 2 warnings (`set-state-in-effect` × 2, same pattern as pre-existing). Bundle delta +176 LOC on QuoteHistory.jsx (1043 → 1219) — net code clarity better despite raw growth because config-driven render eliminates 22 hand-written `<td>` JSX blocks. **Out of scope (raised as follow-ups)**: per-column inline edit, sort-state localStorage persistence (not present today), CSV export for Quote History (no CSV button exists; per-row xlsx ExportModal independent of column visibility — Phase 1's Option B prefix pattern does not apply here). Henry hardware-verified on Mac DMG SERVER 1.5.12 rebuild → admin-merge per Sprint S-D21 culture (CI-red pre-existing per MES-3-FIX-36/37/38 backlog; green checks all passed).

> **Sprint S-D20-COLS-TOGGLE-PHASE-1 — Columns toggle for Cost Breakdown (Summarize) shipped 2026-06-10 (SHA: `3405cd5` via PR #120).** Single PR adds a reusable `<ColumnsToggle>` popover at `client/src/components/Shared/ColumnsToggle.{jsx,css,helpers.js}` + wires it into the Summarize tab's `<ScopedFilterBar>` `rightSlot`. Operator can hide/show any of the 11 cost-breakdown columns (RFQ NO / Direct Customer / End Customer / Production Size / MOQ / Yield% / GM% / VA% / Contr. % / Sale Owner / Update Date) — `rfq_no` flagged `required: true` (checkbox disabled + 0.6 opacity, cannot be hidden). State persists across reload via `localStorage['ops-cost-summarize-cols']`, stale keys (column removed from current config) silently filtered on read. **Helper extraction** (`ColumnsToggle.helpers.js`) — 4 pure functions tested vanilla `node:test` (DesignSyncPicker + CalcLeadTimeNotice pattern): `applyColumnVisibility` (required-guard filter, source-order preserved), `loadHiddenKeys` (storage parse + stale-key drop + corrupt-JSON fallback + SSR-safe), `loadVisibleColumns` (compose helper), `saveHiddenKeys` (sorted-array persist + quota-throw swallow + SSR-safe). 21 helper unit tests cover null/undefined hidden, required-protection regression, stale-key filter, corrupt JSON, no-storage fallback. **CSV Option B (audit prefix + visible cols)** — export prepends `quote_id, tier, update_date, type, sale_owner` then visible columns (deduped). Class prefix `.ct-*` (no collision with DataBrowser `.db-col-*` used by Mfg Structures + IFS Inventory — both legacy toggles coexist without theme drift). Inline SVG icon (3 vertical bars), no Lucide dep first-use. Click-outside via mousedown listener + Escape key close (match ScopedFilterBar DateRangePicker inline pattern). **Two follow-up sticky-header fixes bundled in the same PR** (surfaced during hardware verify on DMG SERVER 1.5.12): commit `b2a72f4` added `--sum-header-h: 36px` CSS var + `top: var(...)` on `.sum-select-header-row > th` to stop the select-hint row from covering the column-label row when both inherit `position: sticky; top: 0` from `.sum-table thead th`; commit `d80bab9` then bumped the selector to `.sum-table thead .sum-select-header-row > th` because the original `.sum-select-header-row > th` had specificity (0,1,1) and lost to `.sum-table thead th` at (0,1,2) — `top: 0` overrode `top: var(--sum-header-h)` until the chain pushed specificity to (0,2,2). Lesson 31 candidate. Bug pre-existing since PR #90 (CSV multi-row hint introduced 2026-05-26) — latent for 4 weeks because operators rarely scrolled the full 126-row Summarize list before this PR made the visible-cols subset more compact. **Tests**: 948/948 client green (927 baseline + 21 new helper unit tests); ESLint clean on 3 new files. Henry hardware-verified all 9 acceptance items (required-column protection, persistence, CSV Option B, regression on Mfg Structures + IFS Inventory + Quote History untouched, sticky-header column + hint rows stacked correctly). Admin-merged via squash per Sprint S-D21 culture (CI-red pre-existing per MES-3-FIX-36/37/38 backlog). **Out of scope**: Phase 2 Quote History columns toggle (separate ticket — table is ~22 columns hardcoded + sort + context menu + status badge + approve/revoke conditional + layout download icon, larger refactor surface).

Single source of truth stays in `processes[i].tool_cost` — no field cache, no drift. Operator chỉnh Tool Cost ở Processes tab → switch sang Lead time tab → cell live-update qua sub-tab unmount/remount (Lesson 18). **6 free-text cells** (`lt_material` / `lt_sample` / `lt_po` / `lt_remark` / `lt_process` / `lt_material_type`) — textarea với `field-sizing: content` (Chromium 123+/Electron 41+) + fallback `min-height: 4.5em`. **Responsive**: CSS Grid 7-col với `minmax()` per column + container query `@container (max-width: 900px)` → stack thành 7 cards (Lesson 11+27 — no viewport media queries). **Schema**: `lead_time: {6 empty strings}` thêm vào `createStdState` + `createEmptyStdState` + `createCplxState` (Cpx có 1 factory dùng chung init + RESET_CPLX, không tách `createEmptyCplxState`). > * **PR #111 — Shared scoped filter bar for Quote History + Cost Breakdown shipped 2026-06-09 (SHA: `7e6cd4c`).** Both
>   tabs fetch the same `sharedApi.getQuotes()` payload with the same `quote.state` schema. Filter logic was duplicated
>   and drifted — **Summarize global search did NOT cover `sale_owner`** (operator regression), and neither tab had
>   Date / Customer / Part / Sale scoped boxes. This change extracts ONE pure filter engine + ONE shared filter-bar UI,
>   preserving the S-PROJFIX (Lesson 21) `end_cu || project` fallback and the NPI quote-level fallback
>   (`state.npi_owner || quote.npi_owner`). New infrastructure: `lib/quoteFilters.js` — pure `applyQuoteFilters()` with
>   caller-provided accessor (identity for Summarize already-flat rows; `quoteAccessor()` helper for raw QuoteHistory
>   quotes). AND-combines across global query + date range + customer + part + sale. 21 test cases cover S-PROJFIX
>   regression + NPI fallback + Summarize sale_owner miss + 4-filter combine + case-insensitive scopes.
>   `hooks/useQuoteFilters.js` — state with built-in 300ms debounce (raw filter drives input value; `debouncedFilter`
>   feeds `applyQuoteFilters`). State in-memory only — tab switch clears (intentional v1; URL sync + sessionStorage
>   persistence deferred to v2). `components/ScopedFilterBar.{jsx,css}` — 2-row filter UI (row 1: global search +
>   rightSlot for tab-specific actions like CSV button or pill filters; row 2: Date popover + 3 scoped text inputs +
>   Clear-all + N-of-M counter). Date popover uses native `<input type="date">` + 5 presets (Today / This week /
>   This month / Last 30 days / Clear) — **no new npm package**. Click-outside + Escape close popover. Tokens.css vars
>   for dark/light theme parity (Lesson 6). Summarize.jsx adds `end_cu: st.end_cu || ''` to row builder for S-PROJFIX
>   fallback. QuoteHistory.jsx pill buttons (All/Standard/Complex/Trash) move into ScopedFilterBar rightSlot;
>   `useEffect` resets page on `debouncedFilter` or `filterType` change. Pagination + sort + sync btn + 30s polling +
>   SSE push intact — filter state lives in hook outside `useAbortableFetch` tick so auto-refresh does NOT reset filter

> **Tech debt + CI state — recorded 2026-06-04 (branch `fix/contribution-reconcile-audit-hardening`, PR #99).** Three CI checks are red on PR #99; classified by re-running each check locally + comparing against `main`'s CI on base commit `30db7d2`: (1) **Lint + format — PRE-EXISTING.** `eslint .` reports 5 errors (no-unused-vars + no-empty), all in files this branch never touched: `client/src/services/connectionHealth.js` (2× unused `_`), `client/src/services/printAreaCore.js` (2× empty block at 1310/1314), `client/src/utils/DecimalInput.jsx` (unused `toDisplay`). Red on `main` too → matches MES-3-FIX-38 backlog. NOT fixed (out of branch scope). (2) **Server tests — PRE-EXISTING.** `npm test` red on CI (Node 22) but green locally on Node 24 — full chain passes (jest no-tests + kiosk vitest 53/53 + `node --test` server/scripts/domains 1231/1231, exit 0). Red on `main` `30db7d2` too, so not branch-caused. Prime suspect: flaky `per-entry ETIMEDOUT is isolated` (`server/routes/backupCode.integration.test.js:113`) or a Node 22↔24 behaviour gap — needs a separate isolated investigation (reproduce under Node 22). NOT fixed. (3) **Commit messages — BRANCH-CAUSED but left as-is.** All 8 commits violate commitlint `body-max-line-length` (120) — bodies were written as single 186–521-char lines via `git commit -m`. Only fixable by rewording history → force-push, which the owner declined (admin-merge squash instead). **New convention going forward: wrap every commit-message body line at ≤120 chars** so the commit-messages check stays green without a force-push.

Operator's "sum visible buckets ≈ subtotal" check failed on every SS/Flexo quote where setups are non-zero (Indigo quotes hid the bug because Indigo setups are 0). Post-fix sum invariant: `TTL.MAT + PROCESS + TOOLING + PACK&SHIP ≈ SUBTOTAL` for typical quotes. > - **PR #90 — CSV export native picker + row selection + RFC 4180 + Production Size column + UTF-8 BOM shipped 2026-05-26 (SHA: `d9045bd`).** Three-stage operator-driven feature build on the Summarize tab CSV Export. **Stage 1**: native Save dialog via File System Access API (`window.showSaveFilePicker`, Chromium 86+ / Electron 13+) — operator picks folder + filename through macOS Save dialog instead of dumping to `~/Downloads/`. Falls back to legacy `<a download>` anchor when API unavailable. New pure helper `client/src/services/csvExport.js` with `buildCsv` + `saveCsv` + RFC 4180 `csvEscape` (doubles embedded `"`, wraps on `[",\n\r]`) — prior `exportCSV` only wrapped on literal `,`, embedded quotes/newlines leaked. **Stage 2**: per-row checkbox column + Select-All header with indeterminate state for partial selection. New `selected` Set state keyed by row `id` (`${quote_id}-${tier}`). Button label dynamic `CSV Export (N)` where N = selected-visible count or all-visible total. Selections persist across filter changes; export = `selected ∩ visible` so a row hidden by filter is never written to disk. Hint row under header shows `N row(s) selected (M more hidden by filter)` if applicable. **Stage 3 (operator follow-ups)**: (a) header alignment fix — initial commit added checkbox cell to data rows but not first header row; switched to `rowSpan={2}` on the select-all TH so it spans both column-label row + hint row, hint TH colSpan adjusted from `columns.length - 1` to `columns.length`. (b) Production Size column added before MOQ — operator screenshot of RFQ-2026-S0013 showed legacy `size` column rendering as `2x3` (canonical `part_width × part_length_md` placeholder); production dimensions actually live in `print_part_width × print_part_length_md`. New `production_size` field sourced from print*part*\* with fallback to canonical. (c) UTF-8 BOM (`﻿`, 3 bytes) prepended to CSV — Excel on Vietnamese / Windows locales detects UTF-8 and decodes `×` correctly (pre-fix showed `220√ó395` because UTF-8 `×` = `0xC3 0x97` mis-read as Windows-1252). (d) `size` column dropped from CSV cols — duplicate of `production_size` for most quotes, confusing on legacy rows. 15 new csvExport tests covering null/undefined/number/empty/comma/quote/newline/combination + BOM-presence + operator-style-row + RFC 4180 regression.
